Every controlled gate does this, so tracing was quadratic: on the 6,000-gate script it took about 2.2s of 6.7s in the profiler, across roughly 18 million comparisons.\n\n**Fix:** it now searches the list from the newest equation backwards, deletes the first match in place and stops. So does the workload script.\n- **Lint:** pylint and black pass on the file.\n\n**Tests:** I ran `tests/capture`, `tests/core`, `tests/ops`, `tests/templates` and `tests/decomposition` on both versions. Neither run passes cleanly: the original code had 251 failures and the patched code had 252.\n- The ones I looked at fail because catalyst isn't installed (`CompileError` on the qjit tests), not because of this change.\n- About 25 failures change between runs in both directions under parallel execution, mostly JAX-interface and capture-disabled tests that never reach this function.\n- Of the 26 that failed only with the patch, 25 pass when rerun one at a time. The last (`test_superposition_thc.py::...test_operation_result[15-2-4-True]`) fails on the same missing-catalyst error before any tracing. It passed in the original-code run, which I haven't explained.\n\nI didn't add a changelog entry to `doc/releases/changelog-dev.md` as `AGENTS.md` asks, because that file is outside `pennylane/pennylane/`, the only place this task allowed changes.
